g_{\text{in}_{x_{i}}},g_{\text{in}_{y_{i}}}
be the gradient after passing through GeLU layer. Then we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g_{\text{in}_{x_{i}}}g_{\text{in}_{y_{i}}}]=

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})g_{\text{out}_{x_{i}}}(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})g_{\text{out}_{y_{i}}}]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g_{\text{in}_{x_{i}}}g_{\text{in}_{y_{i}}}]=\mathbb{E}[(\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+

\displaystyle\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})]\mathbb{E}[g_{\text{out}_{x_{i}}}g_{\text{out}_{y_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+

\displaystyle\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})]r^{l}_{g_{\text{out}}}\sigma_{g_{{\text{out}}}}^{2}

\displaystyle I=\mathbb{E}[(\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+

\displaystyle\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})]

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}(\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+

\displaystyle\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})p_{x_{\text{in}_{i}},y_{\text{in}_{i}}}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

Where
p_{x_{\text{in}_{i}},y_{\text{in}_{i}}}=\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}I_{X}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

Where,

\displaystyle I_{X}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle I_{X,1}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{1}{2}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\exp{(\frac{-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp{(\frac{-(x_{\text{in}_{i}}-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}})^{2}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\exp{(\frac{-(x_{\text{in}_{i}}-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}})^{2}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle I_{X,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}{2}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}

\displaystyle I_{X,2}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}})}{2}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}})\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

From 2.7.1.6 of
Korotkov & Korotkov 2020
,

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{erf}(a_{1}z)\exp{(-az^{2}+bz)}dz=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{a}}\exp{(\frac{b^{2}}{4a})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{a_{1}b}{2\sqrt{a^{2}+aa_{1}^{2}}})

Substituting
a_{1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}},a=\frac{1}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})},b=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}

\displaystyle I_{X,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{\frac{1}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}}\exp{(\frac{\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})^{2}}}{4\frac{1}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}{2\sqrt{\frac{1}{4\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})^{2}}+\frac{1}{4\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}})

\displaystyle I_{X,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}{2}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})

\displaystyle I_{X,3}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)+2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+\frac{2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}{\frac{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-x^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}+\frac{2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}x_{\text{in}_{i}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}{\frac{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})}

\displaystyle\exp{(\frac{\frac{-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{2}}}{\frac{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})}*

\displaystyle\hskip 50.0pt\exp{(\frac{\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{2}}}{\frac{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}*

\displaystyle\hskip 100.0pt\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-(x_{\text{in}_{i}}-\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})^{2}}{\frac{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}\frac{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}}{\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 100.0pt\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\frac{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}}{\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}}\exp{(\frac{-(x_{\text{in}_{i}}-\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})^{2}}{\frac{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})}dx_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}.

\displaystyle\hskip 170.0pt\frac{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}}{\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}

\displaystyle I_{X,3}=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}

\displaystyle I=

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}(I_{X,1}+I_{X,2}+I_{X,3})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle I_{1}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}I_{X,1}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\frac{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}{2}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle I_{1,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}=\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle I_{1,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}})}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}=0

(Integral of odd function)

\displaystyle I_{1,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}=0

(Integral of odd function)

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}I_{X,2}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\frac{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}{2}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}.

\displaystyle\hskip 150.0pt\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle I_{2,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}=0

(Integral of odd function)

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}})\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

From 2.7.1.3 of
Korotkov & Korotkov 2020
,

\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{erf}(a_{1}z)\mathrm{erf}(a_{2}z)\exp{(-az^{2})}dz=\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi a}}\tan^{-1}{(\frac{a_{1}a_{2}}{\sqrt{a^{2}+aa_{1}^{2}+aa_{2}^{2}}})}

Substituting
a=\frac{1}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}},a_{1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}},a_{2}=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi\frac{1}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}}}\tan^{-1}{(\frac{\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{4\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}}+\frac{1}{4\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}+\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{4\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}})}

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\tan^{-1}{(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}+2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}}})}=\frac{1}{2\pi}\tan^{-1}{(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2}}})}

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\sin^{-1}{(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1})}

\displaystyle I_{2,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}})}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}})dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

From 2.6.1.4 of
Korotkov & Korotkov 2020
,

\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}z\mathrm{erf}(az)\exp{(-a_{1}z^{2})}dz=\frac{a}{a_{1}\sqrt{a^{2}+a_{1}}}

Substituting,
a=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}},a_{1}=\frac{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{2,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}\frac{\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{\sqrt{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}}{\frac{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}+\frac{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)\sqrt{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}+2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}}}

\displaystyle I_{2,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2}}}

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}I_{X,3}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\sqrt{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}y_{\text{in}_{i}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}}}{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}\exp{(\frac{(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})}{2(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}(\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}))+\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle I_{3,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{4\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}=0

(Integral of odd function)

\displaystyle I_{3,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{4\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}y_{\text{in}_{i}}\mathrm{erf}(\frac{y_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2}})\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

From 2.6.1.4 of
Korotkov & Korotkov 2020
,

\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}z\mathrm{erf}(az)\exp{(-a_{1}z^{2})}dz=\frac{a}{a_{1}\sqrt{a^{2}+a_{1}}}

Substituting,
a=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}},a_{1}=\frac{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{3,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{4\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}{\frac{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)\sqrt{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}+2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}}}

\displaystyle I_{3,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2}}}

\displaystyle I_{3,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{2})}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4}+2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{4})}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}{\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.0pt\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\frac{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)}}{\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2}}}}\exp{(\frac{-y^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})}{2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)})}dy_{\text{in}_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2\pi\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{3}(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle I_{3,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}

\displaystyle=I_{1,1}+I_{1,2}+I_{1,3}+I_{2,1}+I_{2,2}+I_{2,3}+I_{3,1}+I_{3,2}+I_{3,3}

\displaystyle I=\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\sin^{-1}{(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1})}+

\displaystyle\frac{2r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)\sqrt{(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2}}}+\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{2\pi((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle I=\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\sin^{-1}{(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1})}+\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}((2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+3)(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)-2(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}

We defined
\mathrm{Cov}(g_{\text{in}_{x_{i}}},g_{\text{in}_{y_{i}}})
, as

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(g_{\text{in}_{x_{i}}},g_{\text{in}_{y_{i}}})

\displaystyle=Ir^{l}_{g_{\text{out}}}\sigma_{g_{{\text{out}}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(g_{\text{in}_{x_{i}}},g_{\text{in}_{y_{i}}})=

\displaystyle\left[\frac{1}{4}+\frac{1}{2\pi}\sin^{-1}{(\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1})}+\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}((2\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+3)(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)-2(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})}{2\pi(\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)((\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}+1)^{2}-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right]r^{l}_{g_{\text{out}}}\sigma_{g_{{\text{out}}}}^{2}

A.6
LayerNorm

The affine transformation for layernorm are typically initialized with
1
scale and
0
bias, so they do not change any of our derivations below and are ignored henceforth.
For an input
\mathbf{x_{\text{in}}}
the forward pass of LayerNorm is,

\displaystyle\mathbf{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{LayerNorm}(\mathbf{x_{\text{in}}})

\displaystyle\implies{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}}}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}

Where

\displaystyle{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}({x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}}

To get expectation of output of LayerNorm,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[x_{\text{out}_{i}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\frac{{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}}}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\mathbb{E}[x_{\text{out}_{i}}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\mathbb{E}[\frac{{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}}}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\frac{{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}}}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}({x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\mathbb{E}[x_{\text{out}_{i}}]

\displaystyle=0

By symmetry for any
i,j
and
i\neq j
we have
\mathbb{E}[x_{\text{out}_{i}}]=\mathbb{E}[x_{\text{out}_{j}}]=\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle\implies d_{\text{in}}\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}=0

\displaystyle\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}=0

Similarly we calculate variance of output by,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}({x_{\text{out}_{i}}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[{x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}]-\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]^{2}=\mathbb{E}[{x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\frac{({x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\mathbb{E}[x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\mathbb{E}[\frac{({x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\frac{({x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}({x_{\text{in}_{i}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})^{2}}{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}\mathbb{E}[x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}]

\displaystyle={d_{\text{in}}}

By symmetry for any
i,j
and
i\neq j
we have
\mathbb{E}[x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}]=\mathbb{E}[x^{2}_{\text{out}_{j}}]=\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle\implies d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=d_{\text{in}}

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=1

Now we have
\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}\overset{a.s}{\longrightarrow}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}
for large
d_{\text{in}}
. So for large values of
{d_{\text{in}}}
we can treat
\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}
as a constant which has value
\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}
. We use this approximation to get the following results.
For two inputs
\mathbf{x_{\text{in}}}
and
\mathbf{y_{\text{in}}}
such that for all
i
,
\mathrm{Corr}({x_{\text{in}_{i}}},{y_{\text{in}_{i}}})=r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}
. For all
j
we have,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Corr}({x_{\text{out}_{j}}},{y_{\text{out}_{j}}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{j}}}{y_{\text{out}_{j}}}]-\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{j}}}]\mathbb{E}[{y_{\text{out}_{j}}}]}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}({x_{\text{out}_{j}}})\mathrm{Var}({y_{\text{out}_{j}}})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{j}}}{y_{\text{out}_{j}}}]-\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}}{\sqrt{\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{out}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{out}}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{j}}}{y_{\text{out}_{j}}}]-0}{\sqrt{1}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{j}}}{y_{\text{out}_{j}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\frac{({x_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})({y_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{\bar{y}_{\text{in}}})}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}\hat{\sigma}_{y_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle\approx\mathbb{E}[\frac{({x_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})({y_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{\bar{y}_{\text{in}}})}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[({x_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{\bar{x}_{\text{in}}})({y_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{\bar{y}_{\text{in}}})]}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[({x_{\text{in}_{j}}}-\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{x_{\text{in}_{k}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}})({y_{\text{in}_{j}}}-\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{y_{\text{in}_{l}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}})]}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{in}_{j}}}{y_{\text{in}_{j}}}-{y_{\text{in}_{j}}}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{x_{\text{in}_{k}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}-{x_{\text{in}_{j}}}\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{y_{\text{in}_{l}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}+\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{x_{\text{in}_{k}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{y_{\text{in}_{l}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}]}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}

Elements belonging to different dimensions from
\mathbf{x_{\text{in}}}
and
\mathbf{y_{\text{in}}}
are independent of each other and hence for
i,j
and
i\neq j
we have
\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}{y_{\text{in}_{j}}}]=\mu^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}
.

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{in}_{j}}}{y_{\text{in}_{j}}}]-\mathbb{E}[{y_{\text{in}_{j}}}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{x_{\text{in}_{k}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}]-\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{in}_{j}}}\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{y_{\text{in}_{l}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}]+\mathbb{E}[\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{x_{\text{in}_{k}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}{y_{\text{in}_{l}}}}{{d_{\text{in}}}}]}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}+\mu^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}-\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}+d_{\text{in}}\mu^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{d_{\text{in}}}-\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}+d_{\text{in}}\mu^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{d_{\text{in}}}+\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}+d^{2}_{\text{in}}\mu^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{d^{2}_{\text{in}}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-\frac{1}{d_{\text{in}}})}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Corr}({x_{\text{out}_{j}}},{y_{\text{out}_{j}}})=r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-\frac{1}{d_{\text{in}}})\approx r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}=r^{l}_{x_{\text{out}}}

From
Xu et al. 2019
(Eq. 17), the backward pass through LayerNorm is,

\displaystyle\mathbf{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{g_{{\text{out}}}}}{\hat{\sigma}_{x_{\text{in}}}}(\mathbf{I_{d_{\text{in}}}}-\frac{\mathbf{1^{T}_{d_{\text{in}}}}\mathbf{1_{d_{\text{in}}}}+\mathbf{x^{T}_{\text{out}}}\mathbf{x_{\text{out}}}}{d_{\text{in}}})

\displaystyle\approx\frac{\mathbf{g_{{\text{out}}}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}(\mathbf{I_{d_{\text{in}}}}-\frac{\mathbf{1^{T}_{d_{\text{in}}}}\mathbf{1_{d_{\text{in}}}}+\mathbf{x^{T}_{\text{out}}}\mathbf{x_{\text{out}}}}{d_{\text{in}}})

We have
\displaystyle\lim_{{d_{\text{in}}}\to\infty}\frac{\mathbf{1^{T}_{d_{\text{in}}}}\mathbf{1_{d_{\text{in}}}}+\mathbf{x^{T}_{\text{out}}}\mathbf{x_{\text{out}}}}{d_{\text{in}}}=\mathbf{O_{d_{\text{in}},d_{\text{in}}}}
where
\mathbf{O_{d_{\text{in}},d_{\text{in}}}}
is zero matrix with shape
d_{\text{in}}\times d_{\text{in}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle\approx\frac{\mathbf{g_{{\text{out}}}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}(\mathbf{I_{d_{\text{in}}}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{g_{{\text{out}}}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle\implies g_{{\text{in}_{i}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{{\text{out}_{i}}}}{\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}}

If
\mu_{g_{{\text{out}}}}=0
,

\displaystyle\mu_{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}}{\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}

A.7
Softmax

Assumption
: Other than assuming normally distributed inputs, we also assume that L is large
L>>1
to derive softmax variance.

The forward pass of Softmax can be defined as

\displaystyle\mathbf{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Softmax}(\mathbf{x_{\text{in}}})

\displaystyle{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{x_{\text{in}_{i}}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{L}e^{x_{\text{in}_{j}}}}

For calculating mean we can easily see that,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{L}{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}

\displaystyle=1

Taking expectation both sides, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\sum_{i=1}^{L}{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=1

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=1

By symmetry we can assume that for any
i,j,i\neq j
, we have
\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]=\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{j}}}]

\displaystyle L\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=1

\displaystyle\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}

Let us define
z=\sum_{j}e^{y_{j}}
where
y_{j}=x_{j}-x_{i}
is normally distributed
\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{j})
. Hence, each
e^{y_{j}}
is log-normally distributed, and
z
is a sum of correlated log-normals. Following
(
Lo 2013
)
, this sum of log-normals can be approximated as another log-normal random variable,
Log\mathcal{N}(\mu_{z},\sigma_{z})
, where
\mu_{z}
and
\sigma_{z}
are as follows -

\displaystyle S_{+}

\displaystyle=E[\sum_{j}y_{j}]=\sum_{j}e^{\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2}}{2}}

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{z}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S_{+}^{2}}\sum_{j,k}corr_{j,k}\sigma_{j}\sigma_{k}e^{\frac{1}{2}(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\sigma_{k}^{2})}

\displaystyle\mu_{z}

\displaystyle=ln(S_{+})-\frac{\sigma_{z}^{2}}{2}

Since the difference of two normals
x_{j}
and
x_{i}
is also normal, from the M.G.F. of normal distribution, we have
\sigma_{j}^{2}=2\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})
if
j\neq i
, and
\sigma_{j}^{2}=0
if
j=i
.

Also,
corr_{j,k}=0
if
j=i
or
k=i
, else
corr_{j,k}=\frac{1}{2}
.

We can substitute these values in the above equations, to get

\displaystyle S_{+}

\displaystyle=(L-1)e^{\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})}+1

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{z}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})\frac{L}{L-1}

\displaystyle\mu_{z}

\displaystyle=ln(S_{+})-\frac{\sigma_{z}^{2}}{2}

Since
z
is log-normal,
x_{\text{out}}=\frac{1}{z}
is also log-normal with
Log\mathcal{N}(-\mu_{z},\sigma_{z})
. The variance of log-normal distribution can be obtained from standard formulae for log-normal distribution as
(e^{\sigma_{z}^{2}}-1)e^{\sigma_{z}^{2}-2\mu_{z}}
.

Substituting the values of
\mu_{z}
and
\sigma_{z}
from above, we get

\displaystyle\sigma_{x_{\text{out}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(e^{\sigma_{z}^{2}}-1)e^{2*\sigma_{z}^{2}}}{S_{+}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(e^{\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})\frac{L}{L-1}}-1)e^{2\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})\frac{L}{L-1}}}{((L-1)e^{\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})}+1)^{2}}

For large L, we can ignore the 1 in the denominator -

\displaystyle\sigma_{x_{\text{out}}}^{2}=\frac{(e^{\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}(1-r_{x_{\text{in}}})\frac{L}{L-1}}-1)}{(L-1)^{2}}

If
L>>1
and
\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}
is small, we get the more simplified formula as -

\displaystyle\sigma_{x_{\text{out}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\frac{(e^{(1-r^{d}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}}-1)}{L^{2}}

(Assuming
L>>1
)

Using the mean and variances, we can calculate the scale of softmax output as follows-

\displaystyle E[{x^{2}_{\text{out}}}]

\displaystyle=\sigma_{x_{\text{out}}}^{2}+\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(e^{(1-r^{d}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}})}{L^{2}}

The Jacobian of Softmax can be calculated as (
(
Kim et al. 2021
)
):

\displaystyle J_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}x_{\text{out}_{i}}(1-x_{\text{out}_{i}})&\text{if }i=j\\
-x_{\text{out}_{i}}x_{\text{out}_{j}}&\text{else}\end{cases}

For large values of
L
this approximately becomes

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}

\displaystyle\approx\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{x_{\text{out}}})

\displaystyle\mathbf{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g_{\text{out}}}\mathbf{J}

\displaystyle{g_{\text{in}_{i}}}

\displaystyle\approx{g_{\text{out}_{i}}}{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{g_{\text{in}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle\approx\mathbb{E}[{g_{\text{out}_{i}}}{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[{g_{\text{out}_{i}}}]\mathbb{E}[{x_{\text{out}_{i}}}]=0=\mu_{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{g^{2}_{\text{in}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle\approx\mathbb{E}[{g^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}{x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[{g^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}]\mathbb{E}[{x^{2}_{\text{out}_{i}}}]

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{out}}}\frac{(e^{(1-r^{d}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}})}{L^{2}}

A.8
Scaled Dot-Product Attention

Inapplicability of Direct Usage of Softmax Derivations for SHA
: One may be tempted to assume attention scores to be independent of values. This then enables the use of our previous LogNormal-based softmax derivation, to easily derive the forward variances.

But the theoretically calculated moments strongly disagree with empirical simulations. This is because SHA is

\mathbf{X_{\text{out}}}=\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathrm{SoftMax}(\dfrac{\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{Q}}\mathbf{{W_{K}}^{T}}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}}}{\sqrt{d_{k}}}))\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}
, and the
\mathbf{{W_{K}}^{T}}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}}
term cannot be treated independently of the
\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}
term. A simple verification of this can be checked by simply simulating
\mathbf{(X{W}^{T}})X
, and verifying that the variances of the results do not match that of
L*\sigma^{2}(\mathbf{(XW)})
, but do if the second
\mathbf{X}
is replaced by another random tensor.

This necessitates an alternate methodology to derive SHA, where the components are treated as a unified whole.

Assumption
: We assume that
L
and
d_{\text{in}}
are very large when compared to scale of scores being passed to the Softmax. These approximations hold true for small values of
\sigma_{q}
and
\sigma_{k}
, and the resulting formulae are fairly accurate, as shown in the numerical verification section.

The forward pass of Scaled Dot-Product Attention is

\displaystyle\mathbf{X_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathrm{SoftMax}(\frac{\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{K^{T}}}{\sqrt{d_{i,k}}}))\mathbf{V}

Where,

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{Q}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{K}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{X_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathrm{SoftMax}(\frac{\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{Q}}\mathbf{{W_{K}}^{T}}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}}}{\sqrt{d_{i,k}}}))\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}

Let,

\displaystyle\mathbf{O}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathrm{SoftMax}(\frac{\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{Q}}\mathbf{{W_{K}}^{T}}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}}}{\sqrt{d_{i,k}}}))\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{Q}}\mathbf{{W_{K}}^{T}}}{\sqrt{d_{i,k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{O}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathrm{SoftMax}(\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}}))\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}

Using results from Linear Layer we have
\sigma^{2}_{w}=d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}=d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk}

\displaystyle O_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathrm{SoftMax}(\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}}))_{i,k}{X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathrm{Dropout}(\frac{\exp{((\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}})_{i,k}})}{\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{L}\exp{((\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}})_{i,m}})}){X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{L}\frac{\mathrm{Dropout}(\exp{((\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}})_{i,k}}))}{\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{L}\exp{((\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}})_{i,m}})}{X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathrm{Dropout}(\exp{((\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}})_{i,k}})){X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}}}{\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{L}\exp{((\mathbf{W}\mathbf{X^{T}_{\text{in}}})_{i,m}})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathrm{Dropout}(\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}X_{\text{in}_{k,l}})}){X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}}}{\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{n=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,n}X_{\text{in}_{m,n}})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathrm{Dropout}(\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}X_{\text{in}_{k,l}})}{X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}})}{\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{n=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,n}X_{\text{in}_{m,n}})}}

Each
X_{\text{in}_{i,j}}
can be written as:

\displaystyle X_{\text{in}_{i,j}}

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{j}+\delta_{i,j}

Where
\epsilon_{j}
and
\delta_{i,j}
are all independent and defined as

\displaystyle\epsilon_{j}

\displaystyle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\delta_{i,j}

\displaystyle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})

\displaystyle O_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathrm{Dropout}(\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}X_{\text{in}_{k,l}})}{X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}})}{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}X_{\text{in}_{k,l}})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})(\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}X_{\text{in}_{k,l}})}{X_{\text{in}_{k,j}}})}{(1-p)\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}X_{\text{in}_{k,l}})}}

Where
d_{i,k}
is Bernoulli random variable which is 1 with probability
p

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}(\epsilon_{l}+\delta_{k,l}))}(\epsilon_{j}+\delta_{k,j})}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}(\epsilon_{l}+\delta_{k,l}))}}

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{j}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\epsilon_{l})}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\epsilon_{l})}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\epsilon_{l})}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\epsilon_{l})}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{j}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}+\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}

Let
v_{1}=\epsilon_{j}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}
and
v_{2}=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}
. We have,

\displaystyle O_{i,j}

\displaystyle=v_{1}+v_{2}

Given a fixed
\epsilon,W
, we have

\displaystyle v_{1}|\epsilon,W

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{j}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{j}\frac{\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}}{(1-p)\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}}

By WLLN,
\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}{\overset{p}{\to}}(1-p)\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}]
, and

(1-p)\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}{\overset{p}{\to}}(1-p)\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}]

Thus, we have
v_{1}|\epsilon,W{\overset{p}{\to}}\epsilon_{j}

\displaystyle v_{2}|\epsilon,W

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\frac{1}{\sqrt{L}}\sqrt{L}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\frac{(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{L}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}}

Let
\mu_{\text{num}}=\mathbb{E}_{\delta,d}[(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}]
,
\sigma_{\text{num}}^{2}=\mathrm{Var}_{\delta,d}((1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j})
. By central limit theorem for large
L
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{L}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{L}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{L}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})(\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}-\mu_{\text{num}})}{L}+\sqrt{L}\mu_{\text{num}}

\displaystyle\sqrt{L}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{L}

\displaystyle{\overset{d}{\to}}\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{\text{num}}^{2})+\sqrt{L}\mu_{\text{num}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{L}

\displaystyle{\overset{d}{\to}}\mathcal{N}(\mu_{\text{num}},\frac{\sigma_{\text{num}}^{2}}{L})

\displaystyle\mu_{\text{num}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{d}[1-d_{i,k}](\prod_{l=1,l\neq j}^{l=d}\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}])\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(W_{i,j}\delta_{k,j})}\delta_{k,j}]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}]

\displaystyle=\exp{(\frac{W_{i,l}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}

(MGF of gaussian)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(W_{i,j}\delta_{k,j})}\delta_{k,j}]

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\exp{(W_{i,j}\delta_{k,j})}\delta_{k,j}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{\delta}}\exp{(-\frac{\delta_{k,j}^{2}}{2\sigma_{\delta}^{2}})}d\delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp{(\frac{W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}\frac{\delta_{k,j}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{\delta}}\exp{(-\frac{(\delta_{k,j}-W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})^{2}}{2\sigma_{\delta}^{2}})}d\delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle=\exp{(\frac{W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\delta_{k,j}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{\delta}}\exp{(-\frac{(\delta_{k,j}-W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})^{2}}{2\sigma_{\delta}^{2}})}d\delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle=\exp{(\frac{W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle\mu_{\text{num}}

\displaystyle=(1-p)\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{num}}^{2}=\mathbb{E}_{d}[(1-d_{i,k})^{2}]

\displaystyle(\prod_{l=1,l\neq j}^{l=d}\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(2W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}]\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(2W_{i,j}\delta_{k,j})}\delta_{k,j}^{2}]-\mu_{\text{num}}^{2})

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\delta_{k,l}}[\exp{(2W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}]

\displaystyle=\exp{(2W_{i,l}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}

(MGF of gaussian)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\delta_{k,j}}[\exp{(2W_{i,j}\delta_{k,j})}\delta_{k,j}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\exp{(2W_{i,j}\delta_{k,j})}\delta_{k,j}^{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{\delta}}\exp{(-\frac{\delta_{k,j}^{2}}{2\sigma_{\delta}^{2}})}d\delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp{(2W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}\frac{\delta_{k,j}^{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{\delta}}\exp{(-\frac{(\delta_{k,j}-2W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})^{2}}{2\sigma_{\delta}^{2}})}d\delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle=\exp{(2W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\delta_{k,j}^{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{\delta}}\exp{(-\frac{(\delta_{k,j}-2W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})^{2}}{2\sigma_{\delta}^{2}})}d\delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle=\exp{(2W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}(4W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{num}}^{2}=(1-p)\exp{(2\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}

\displaystyle(4W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})-(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}

Similarly,
\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}
is also a sum of
L
i.i.d. random variables for fixed
W
. By WLLN we have,

\displaystyle(1-p)\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}

\displaystyle{\overset{p}{\to}}(1-p)\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}]

\displaystyle{\overset{p}{\to}}(1-p)(\prod_{l=1}^{l=d}\mathbb{E}_{\delta}[\exp{(W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}])

\displaystyle(1-p)\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}

\displaystyle{\overset{p}{\to}}(1-p)\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}

\displaystyle v_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{L}}{\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{L}}

As for a given
W,\epsilon
, both the numerator and denominator converge in distribution and denominator is converging to a constant by Slutskys theorem,

\displaystyle v_{2}|W,\epsilon

\displaystyle{\overset{d}{\to}}\mathcal{N}(\frac{\mu_{\text{num}}}{(1-p)\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}},\frac{\sigma_{\text{num}}^{2}}{L(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}})

\displaystyle v_{2}|W,\epsilon

\displaystyle{\overset{d}{\to}}\mathcal{N}(W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2},\frac{\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}(4W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{(1-p)}-W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L})

Thus we have,

\displaystyle O_{i,j}|W,\epsilon

\displaystyle\sim\mathcal{N}(W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2},\frac{\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}(4W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{(1-p)}-W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L})+\epsilon_{j}

We have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}|W]

\displaystyle=W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}+0=W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O^{2}_{i,j}|W]

\displaystyle=\frac{\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}(4W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{(1-p)}-W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{W}[O_{i,j}|W]=\mathbb{E}_{W}[W_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}]=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{W}[O_{i,j}^{2}|W]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{W}[W^{2}_{i,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\frac{\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}(4W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{(1-p)}-W_{i,j}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}]

For large
d_{\text{in}}
by WLLN and continuous mapping theorem
\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}\approx\exp{(d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}

\displaystyle=\frac{(L-1)\sigma_{w}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\frac{\exp{(d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}(4\sigma_{w}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{(1-p)}}{L}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}(L-1)d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk}+\frac{\exp{((1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})d^{2}_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk})}(4(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})}{(1-p)}}{L}+r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}

Hence,

\displaystyle\mu_{x_{out}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{x_{out}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}(L-1)d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk}+\frac{\exp{((1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})d^{2}_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk})}(4(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})}{(1-p)}}{L}+r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}

Now to get covariance we make two approximations. As the term
\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}{(1-p)\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}}
converges to 1, we approximate
v_{1_{i,j}}\approx\epsilon_{j}
. Also we will treat
\sum_{k=1}^{L}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\approx\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle v_{1_{i,j}}

\displaystyle\approx\epsilon_{j}

\displaystyle v_{2_{i,j}}

\displaystyle\approx\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\frac{(1-d_{i,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k,l})}\delta_{k,j}}{L}}{(1-p)\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W^{2}_{i,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

This makes
v_{1_{i,j}}
and
v_{2_{i,j}}
independent. For covariance

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{W}[\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}|W]]

\displaystyle O_{i,j}O_{m,j}|W

\displaystyle=(v_{1_{i,j}}+v_{2_{i,j}})(v_{1_{m,j}}+v_{2_{m,j}})

\displaystyle=v_{1_{i,j}}v_{1_{m,j}}+v_{1_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}+v_{2_{i,j}}v_{1_{m,j}}+v_{2_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}

\displaystyle v_{1_{i,j}}v_{1_{m,j}}

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{1_{i,j}}v_{1_{m,j}}|W]

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}

As
v_{1_{i,j}}=v_{1_{m,j}}=\epsilon_{j},v_{1_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}+v_{2_{i,j}}v_{1_{m,j}}=\epsilon_{j}(v_{2_{i,j}}+v_{2_{m,j}})
, and
\epsilon_{j}
is independent of
(v_{2_{i,j}}+v_{2_{m,j}})
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{1_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}+v_{2_{i,j}}v_{1_{m,j}}|W]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{j}|W]\mathbb{E}[(v_{2_{i,j}}+v_{2_{m,j}})|W]=0*\mathbb{E}[(v_{2_{i,j}}+v_{2_{m,j}})|W]=0

\displaystyle v_{2_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{L}\frac{(1-d_{i,k_{1}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k_{1},l})}\delta_{k_{1},j}}{L}\sum_{k_{2}=1}^{L}\frac{(1-d_{m,k_{2}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{m,l}\delta_{k_{2},l})}\delta_{k_{2},j}}{L}}{(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{2_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}|W]=\frac{\mathbb{E}[\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k_{1}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k_{1},l})}\delta_{k_{1},j}\sum_{k_{2}=1}^{L}(1-d_{m,k_{2}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{m,l}\delta_{k_{2},l})}\delta_{k_{2},j}]}{L^{2}(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

Breaking summation into two parts:
k_{1}=k_{2}=k
and
k_{1}\neq k_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[\sum_{k=1}^{L}(1-d_{i,k})(1-d_{m,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}+W_{m,l})\delta_{k,l})}\delta^{2}_{k,j}]}{L^{2}(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\mathbb{E}[\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{L}\sum_{k_{2}=1,k_{2}\neq k_{1}}^{L}(1-d_{i,k_{1}})(1-d_{m,k_{2}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k_{1},l})}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{m,l}\delta_{k_{2},l})}\delta_{k_{1},j}\delta_{k_{2},j}]}{L^{2}(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{i,k})(1-d_{m,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}+W_{m,l})\delta_{k,l})}\delta^{2}_{k,j}]}{L^{2}(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{L}\sum_{k_{2}=1,k_{2}\neq k_{1}}^{L}\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{i,k_{1}})(1-d_{m,k_{2}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k_{1},l})}\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{m,l}\delta_{k_{2},l})}\delta_{k_{1},j}\delta_{k_{2},j}]}{L^{2}(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{i,k})(1-d_{m,k})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}+W_{m,l})\delta_{k,l})}\delta^{2}_{k,j}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{i,k})]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{m,k})]\mathbb{E}[\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}+W_{m,l})\delta_{k,l})}\delta^{2}_{k,j}]

\displaystyle=(1-p)^{2}

\displaystyle\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}+W_{m,l})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}((W_{i,j}+W_{m,j})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{i,k_{1}})(1-d_{m,k_{2}})\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k_{1},l})}

\displaystyle\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{m,l}\delta_{k_{2},l})}\delta_{k_{1},j}\delta_{k_{2},j}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{i,k_{1}})]\mathbb{E}[(1-d_{m,k_{2}})]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}\delta_{k_{1},l})}\delta_{k_{1},j}]\mathbb{E}[\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{m,l}\delta_{k_{2},l})}\delta_{k_{2},j}]

\displaystyle=(1-p)^{2}\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}^{2}+W_{m,l}^{2})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}W_{i,j}W_{m,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[v_{2_{i,j}}v_{2_{m,j}}|W]

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}+W_{m,l})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}((W_{i,j}+W_{m,j})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{L\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(L-1)\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W_{i,l}^{2}+W_{m,l}^{2})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}W_{i,j}W_{m,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L\exp{(\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}(W^{2}_{i,l}+W^{2}_{m,l})\sigma_{\delta}^{2}}{2})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}W_{m,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}((W_{i,j}+W_{m,j})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{L}+\frac{(L-1)W_{i,j}W_{m,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L}

So, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}|W]

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}+\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}W_{m,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}((W_{i,j}+W_{m,j})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{L}+\frac{(L-1)W_{i,j}W_{m,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{W}[\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}|W]]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{W}[\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}+\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}W_{m,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}((W_{i,j}+W_{m,j})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{L}+\frac{(L-1)W_{i,j}W_{m,j}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}}{L}]

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{W}[\frac{\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}W_{m,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}((W_{i,j}+W_{m,j})^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{L}]

For large values of
d_{\text{in}}
by WLLN and continuous mapping theorem we have
\exp{(\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{in}}}W_{i,l}W_{m,l}\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}\approx 1
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}]

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}+\frac{(2\sigma_{w}^{2}\sigma_{\delta}^{4}+\sigma_{\delta}^{2})}{L}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[O_{i,j}O_{m,j}]

\displaystyle=r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}+\frac{(2(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{qk}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})}{L}

The convergence arguments we have made require the scale of the variables to be small when compared to
L
and
d_{\text{in}}
. The growth in scale can be controlled easily by controlling
\sigma_{qk}
, and we observe that if we let
\sigma_{qk}
become arbitrarily large the scores passed to Softmax diverge leading to degenerate attention only attending to one token which has the highest score. To avoid this degenerate attention, we choose smaller values of
\sigma_{q},\sigma_{k}
and in that scenario, the approximate value for variance and covariance are,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{x_{out}}

\displaystyle\approx r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{x_{out}}

\displaystyle\approx r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}

To get the final variance and covariance we can use results of Linear layer to account for
\mathbf{W_{V}}
.
If we initialize
\sigma_{q}
and
\sigma_{k}
to be small, in initial phase of training the output of Softmax layer can be treated as being a constant
=\mathbf{\frac{1^{T}_{L}1_{L}}{L}}
. Using this assumption we have,

\displaystyle\mathbf{X_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle\approx\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathbf{\frac{1^{T}_{L}1_{L}}{L}})\mathbf{X_{\text{in}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}

\displaystyle\implies\mathbf{g_{X_{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle\approx\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathbf{\frac{1^{T}_{L}1_{L}}{L}})^{T}\mathbf{g_{X_{\text{out}}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}^{T}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Dropout}(\mathbf{\frac{1^{T}_{L}1_{L}}{L}})\mathbf{g_{X_{\text{out}}}}\mathbf{W_{V}}^{T}

\displaystyle\mu_{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}d\sigma^{2}_{v}}{L(1-p)}(1+(L-1)r^{l}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}(1-p))

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{g_{{\text{in}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}d\sigma^{2}_{v}}{L}(1+(L-1)r^{l}_{g_{{\text{out}}}})

Appendix B
Moment Propagation through Transformer Blocks

B.1
Transformer Attention Block

A forward pass through attention block consists of LayerNorm, followed by Scaled Dot-Product Attention, followed by an output projection layer (a Linear Layer), and finally a Dropout. Using the results from above we get,

\displaystyle\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}=0*0*0*0=0

\displaystyle\sigma_{x_{\text{out}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}(L-1)d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+\frac{\exp{((1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})d^{2}_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k})}(4(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})}{(1-p)}}{L}+r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}\right).d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{v}.\frac{d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{o}}{(1-p)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{o}\sigma^{2}_{v}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}}{(1-p)}\left(\frac{(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}(L-1)d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+\frac{\exp{((1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})d^{2}_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k})}(4(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}))}{(1-p)}}{L}+r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=\left(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{in}}}+\frac{(2(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{6}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})}{L}\right).d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{v}.d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{o}.1

\displaystyle=d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{o}\sigma^{2}_{v}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\left(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}+\frac{(2(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}))}{L}\right)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{out}}}*\frac{1}{(1-p)}*d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{o}^{2}*\frac{d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{v}}{L(1-p)}(1+(L-1)r^{l}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}(1-p))

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}\sigma^{2}_{v}\sigma^{2}_{o}}{L(1-p)^{2}}(1+(L-1)r^{l}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}(1-p))

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{out}}}*1*d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{o}^{2}*\frac{d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{2}_{v}}{L}(1+(L-1)r^{l}_{g_{{\text{out}}}})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{g_{{\text{out}}}}\sigma^{2}_{v}\sigma^{2}_{o}}{L}(1+(L-1)r^{l}_{g_{{\text{out}}}})

B.2
Transformer FFN Block

A forward pass through the FFN block of a transfer has a LayerNorm, then a Linear layer from
d
to
4d
, which is then passed through a ReLU gate, the output of which is the projected back to
d
dimension using another Linear layer, and eventually passed through a Dropout. Again using the results from above we get,

\displaystyle\mu_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=0

(Last Linear Layer makes it 0)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=1*d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}*(\frac{\pi-1}{2\pi}+\frac{1}{2\pi})*4d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}*\frac{1}{(1-p)}*\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}}{(1-p)}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}*(\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{4}+\dfrac{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})^{0.5}}{2\pi}+\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sin^{-1}(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})}{2\pi}-\dfrac{1}{2\pi}+\dfrac{1}{2\pi})*4d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}*\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=4d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}(\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{4}+\dfrac{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})^{0.5}}{2\pi}+\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sin^{-1}(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})}{2\pi})

\displaystyle\mathrm{r}^{l}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\displaystyle=2*(1-p)*(\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{4}+\dfrac{(1-(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2})^{0.5}}{2\pi}+\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sin^{-1}(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})}{2\pi})

\displaystyle\approx(1-p)*(\dfrac{r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}{2}+\dfrac{1}{\pi}+(\dfrac{1}{2}-\dfrac{1}{\pi}){r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}}^{2})

(Fitting a 2-nd order polynomial)

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}_{g_{\text{out}}}*\frac{1}{(1-p)}*d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}*\frac{1}{2}*4d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{2d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}\sigma_{g_{\text{out}}}^{2}}{(1-p)}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{g_{\text{in}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{g_{\text{out}}}*1*d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}*(\dfrac{1}{4}+\dfrac{\sin^{-1}(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})}{2\pi})*4d_{\text{in}}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=4d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{g_{\text{out}}}(\dfrac{1}{4}+\dfrac{\sin^{-1}(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})}{2\pi})

\dfrac{2d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{1}}^{2}\sigma_{w_{2}}^{2}\sigma_{g_{\text{out}}}^{2}}{(1-p)}

Covariance (along sequence length) propagation through the components of transformer model.

Component

\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{x_{\text{out}}}

\sum{\dfrac{N_{i}*(N_{i}-1)}{L*(L-1))}}*\sigma_{w_{\text{embd}}}^{2}

)

d_{1}\sigma_{w}^{2}(\mathrm{Cov}^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}+\mu_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2})

Attn Block (Approx)

d_{\text{in}}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{o}\sigma^{2}_{v}\sigma_{x_{\text{in}}}^{2}\left(r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}+\frac{(2(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}})^{2}d_{\text{in}}\sigma^{4}_{x_{\text{in}}}\sigma^{2}_{q}\sigma^{2}_{k}+(1-r^{l}_{x_{\text{in}}}))}{L}\right)

50_{th}
